## leader_trait_reckless_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_reckless_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该[owner.GetIndividualName]在舰队作战时常常错误估算当前的风险，当理论上应该撤退、修复、重新集结的时候，它反而会选择继续战斗。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_4_l_simp_chinese.yml:42`

### English

This [owner.GetIndividualName] miscalculates risks, continuing to fight when the logical thing would be to retreat, repair, and regroup.

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_4_l_english.yml:53`

## leader_trait_par_zealot_hive_desc

Namespace: `gameplay` · Status: `paired` · [Viewer](https://wangyufeng.org/stellaris/?entry=gameplay%3Aleader_trait_par_zealot_hive_desc&lang=en)

### 中文

该[owner.GetIndividualName]对我们继续生存的奉献，与它对了解敌人动机和驱动力的渴望相匹配，目的是学习和提高[owner.GetGestaltEntityName]的能力。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_4_l_simp_chinese.yml:188`

### English

This [owner.GetIndividualName]'s dedication to our continued survival is matched only by its thirst for understanding our enemies' motives and driving forces, for the purpose of learning and advancing the capabilities of the [owner.GetGestaltEntityName].

Source: `localisation/english/paragon_4_l_english.yml:236`

### English

## leader_trait_par_zealot_machine_desc

### 中文

该单元设计用于分析敌对物种的动机和驱动力，以确保集体意识的生存与繁荣。

Source: `localisation/simp_chinese/paragon_4_l_simp_chinese.yml:190`

### English

This unit is designed to analyze the motives and driving forces of hostile species, in order to ensure the survival and prosperity of the consciousness.
